Bolt (霆) damage calculation audit

Generated 9 November 2026 from the current dirty workspace. This describes the implemented calculation for independent datamine review; exact reconciliation proves that the report matches the simulator, not that every game interpretation is correct.

Fully Relayed Max Build · BT19 · 寒芒 4 · Dummy 1 Min · 40 ms ping
月湧方圓 / 神斷青鋒 / 浮梁一夜 / 明非通判, all T6. Simmering Fish Slices, no Script, Fire Divinecraft. Default global debuffs are all inactive. Target: level 96, defense 500, all resistances 0, judgement resistance 0.85. No skill overrides.

74,016.131548 DPS; 4,440,967.892884 damage / 60 s. All 426 contact rows were re-evaluated through the production formula and matched with maximum per-contact difference 0. 837 resolved probability leaves were traced. Mystic Vitality factor = 1.

Companion machine-readable artifact: bolt-damage-calculation-2026-11-09.json. It contains the full runtime input bundle, build gear, all coefficients, every contact and probability leaf, resolved stats/effects, outcome channel results, exact floats, and source hashes.

Questions for the datamine agent

  1. Confirm Normal CN layer-one, level-100 per-hit coefficients and flat terms, callback counts, and NPC ratio corrections. Ordinary VC +30%, 鋒銳 VC +50%, 鳴戈 +60%, and 鋒銳 T6 +30% currently add in the base-ratio pool. Check whether these belong to the same additive ratio adjustment or require a different composition.
  2. Confirm native event-34 selectors 1/2 for no-flat talents. Runtime multiplies the 1.8 鳴戈 coefficient by 1.015 = 1.827 (and 0.276 載戈 by 1.015), rather than adding 0.015 to the coefficient. Verify the native consumer, not tooltip wording alone.
  3. Confirm 鳴戈 receives 鋒銳, 載戈 10% × stacks, max-Physical talent, full 通判 penetration, and guaranteed Affinity at detonation; confirm source-owner checks and early-Tab/expiry consumption order. All four current detonations observe 15 載戈 stacks and 鋒銳.
  4. Confirm 泉威 = 8% Physical/Bellstrike generally +6% for VC series with 浮梁一夜 T3; blue attack talent applies during 鋒銳 OR to 鳴戈; both are channel bonuses. Check whether the source conditions intentionally admit Flute, Moon procs and Divinecraft.
  5. Confirm HanMang 4, cross-VC buffs, Moon Pull and 載戈 are Category 1; 月湧方圓 T6 DAMAGE_UP is also Category 1 rather than base/Mechanism. Check interaction with existing boss/martial/weapon-art bonuses.
  6. Confirm active-art behavior: shared Blue/Red VC preserves the wielded spear; native Q/Special/Tab changes it. Attunement covers primary VC only, while series bonuses include 載戈/鳴戈. Check the exact native tags.
  7. Review contact schedules and omitted alternatives: 240000113 threefold red-VC branch requires its own buff; unactivated first-light C3 and unsupported red basic-chain identities are not invented. Published animation/contact clocks remain audit-provisional.
  8. Review approved expected approximations separately from native mechanics: expected 月引 extension uses 2 × Affinity chance per qualifying hit; expected 潮湧 is aligned to whole battle seconds; tiny inactive 月相 stack-count mass below 1e-5 promotes upward. Native sampled extensions/timers remain separate.

Defensive/control-only effects and incoming attack classification are outside this outgoing-Dummy-DPS report. No live-game validation or claim of exact native network timing is made.

Exact formula structure used

For each resolved probability leaf and outcome o:
  Cp = authored physical coefficient × (1 + no-flat talent), only if BOTH flat terms are zero
  Ca = authored attribute coefficient × the same no-flat factor
  Fp/Fa = authored flat terms × (1 + flat-attack talent)
  penetration(P,R) = 1 + (P−R)/200 if P ≥ R, otherwise 1 + (P−R)/100
  A_o = min attack for Abrasion; mean for Normal/Critical; max for Affinity
  channel raw Physical = [Cp × (A_o × attack bonus − adjusted defense) + Fp]
                         × physical penetration × (1 + physical channel bonus)
  channel raw Bellstrike = [Ca × Bellstrike A_o × attack bonus + Fa]
                           × Bellstrike penetration × (1 + Bellstrike channel bonus) × 1.5
  shared Physical = damageScale × (1 + base bonus) × (1 + Category 1) × (1 + attunement)
  shared Attribute uses Category 1 + attributeDMGBonus in that same bracket
  outcome Physical = max(0, raw Physical × shared Physical × outcome factor × DOT factor × Mechanism)
  outcome Bellstrike = raw Bellstrike × shared Attribute × outcome factor × DOT factor
                       × (Mechanism + Bellstrike Mechanism bonus)
  outcome factor = 1 for Abrasion/Normal, 1 + critical bonus for Critical, 1 + Affinity bonus for Affinity
  Critical bonus clamp: 35%..150%; Affinity bonus clamp: 20%..130%
  leaf damage = Σ(outcome chance × outcome channel damage)
  contact damage = Σ(leaf probability × leaf damage)
  rotation damage = Σ(contact damage × applicable Mystic Vitality factor)
  DPS = rotation damage / 60

damageScale already contains an emitted proc’s probability. hitProbability is shown for expected counts and trigger admission; it is not multiplied into damage a second time. All four attribute channels are evaluated; this build has nonzero Bellstrike only. Void attack is folded into effective Bellstrike before hit evaluation. Defense is subtracted inside the coefficient term, while flat attack is added afterward. Physical damage is clamped per outcome before averaging.

Category 1 = boss bonus + eligible all-Martial-Arts + eligible weapon art + eligible Mystic bonuses + active dmgBonus/hpDMGBonus. The character’s Physical/Bellstrike channel bonuses are separate multiplicative brackets. Mechanism = 1 + globalDmgBonus + globalHPDMGBonus; Bellstrike can add globalBellstrikeDMGBonus. Exhausted contributes +10% Mechanism.

Expected Precision = min(1, (sheet Precision − 0.65)/(1 + 0.85) + 0.65). Effective Crit = min(0.8, sheet Crit / 1.85 + effective Crit bonus). Effective Affinity = min(0.4, sheet Affinity / 1.85). Final Affinity adds Direct Affinity. Crit chance = min(effective Crit + capped Direct Crit, 1 − final Affinity) × effective Precision. Abrasion = (1 − final Affinity) × (1 − effective Precision); Normal receives the remainder. 鋒銳 NoAbrasion sets effective Precision to 1 for matching hits; guaranteed Affinity sets its chance to 1.

Red max-Physical talent min(floor(action maxPhys / 100) × 1.2%, 18%) Affinity Damage on full VC series. Current build reaches 18%.
Blue max-Physical talent min(floor(action maxPhys / 125) × 1%, 12%) Physical/Bellstrike channel bonuses during 鋒銳 OR for 鳴戈. Current build reaches 12%.
Blue Bellstrike conversion min(raw maxBellstrike × 0.000168, 12.6%) Bellstrike channel bonus, already in sheet. Red penetration conversion caps at 25.2 points.
Flat attack / no-flat talent 15% matching current-art flat bonus; 1.5% coefficient multiplier on explicit no-flat effects 15% scales flat terms only. 載戈/鳴戈 have no flats and use the no-flat coefficient enhancement across weapon swaps.
神斷青鋒 T0 / T6 +5% Affinity Damage while red; during 鋒銳 both weapons receive it Series only. Leech is recovery and does not add outgoing damage.
鋒銳 + red talent / 神斷 T6 +30% base bonus at T6; +10% Affinity Damage; NoAbrasion Series only; base bonus joins existing skill NPC base correction.
明法 / 重道 Next opposite VC +10% HP damage; another +10% during 鋒銳 at T3 Category 1. Applies to the primary opposite VC tags, not every shared-series proc.
浮梁 T4 王行 +3% Direct Affinity against supported boss/Dummy targets Chance, not Affinity Damage.
明非通判 T6 Base +5 penetration; +1 per 通判 stack, plus +2 per stack for VC series 5 stacks give 20 total Physical/all-attribute penetration for VCs/載戈/鳴戈, 10 for other damage. Exact chance distributions are retained.
泉威 / 浮梁 T3 +8% Physical/Bellstrike generally; another +6% for VC series Channel bonuses, not Category 1. Series total 14%; primary VC hits extend the target debuff by 2 s.
載戈 stacks 10% × 1..15 stacks Category 1 for 鳴戈 only; 15 stacks = +150%, not a separate ×2.5 bracket on top of Category 1.
月引 +5% HP damage and +1.5% Direct Affinity Each probability leaf either contains its full bonus or does not. Stats are not averaged before the nonlinear formula.
月湧 T3 Additional +1.5% Direct Affinity while 月引 is active At stable uptime, +3% Direct Affinity total from the Moon branches; with sheet 44.6% and 王行 3%, typical final Affinity is 50.6%.
月湧 T6 min(floor(effectiveAffinity / 1%) × 1%, 30%) Category 1 for 潮湧/洪波萬仞, at cap in this build.
Flute Distance-segment HP damage bonus while Flute is active Category 1; the 1 m default resolves to 2% under current LowerBoundInclusive lookup. Review this segment convention independently.
Exhausted +10% global damage Mechanism, separate from Category 1; active snapshots are attached to every contact.
VC attunement 4 × 7% = 28% Standalone ×1.28 on primary light/heavy VC, both ordinary and 鋒銳; 載戈/鳴戈 and Q/Special do not inherit it.

All named rules and their exact machine predicates are included in runtimeBundle.timeline.setupEffects, innerWayRules, effectDefinitions, and skill modifiers in the JSON. Per-contact leaves record the rules that actually passed, plus aggregate fields. Zero-value/non-damage trigger metadata remains available there.

Machine-readable navigation

Refs are zero-based indices into pools. For each contact, sum(branch.probability * pools.traces[traceRef].result.total), then multiply vitalityScale. action.damageScale already includes any emitted-event probability; do not multiply hitProbability again.
trace.contextRef → pools.contexts
context.statsRef / derivedStatsRef → pools.stats
context.effectsRef → pools.effects
context.aggregateRef → pools.aggregates
trace.outcomes order = [Abrasion, Normal, Critical, Affinity]
The context contains fully resolved ordinary/effective stats for that leaf.
contact.inputExpectedEffects retains the original full distribution.
sourceFiles pin implementation/data; runtimeBundle pins the complete calculation inputs.
